Intrusion
Detection &
Prevention
UniFi’s IDS/IPS guards against potential threats inside and outside the network. This system swiftly detects and blocks suspicious activity that may signal compromise, using a real-time database of known threats.
Advanced
Segmentation
Smarter security, less complexity. UniFi’s industry-standard Zone-Based Firewall groups networks into LAN, WAN, Guest, and IoT zones so you set policies at scale, not hundreds of rules - streamlining security, reducing errors, and simplifying management.
Application-
Aware
Firewall
Accurately detects and blocks traffic directed at specific applications, websites, and IP addresses. Easily block specific targets that might pose security threats at the network, VLAN, and client device level.
Massive Scale
SD-WAN
UniFi’s license-free Site Magic makes Site-to-Site VPN and SD-WAN setup easier. Instead of complex manual VPNs between gateways, simply choose sites and subnets in UniFi Site Manager at unifi.ui.com.
Single-Click
VPN & Zero
Trust WiFi
UniFi Endpoint simplifies secure network and physical access. Easily manage user permissions for WiFi, VPN, and doors.
Securely connect users to company networks and resources without manual setup, WiFi passwords, or VPN configuration nightmares.
BGP + OSPF
Dynamic
Routing
Full-table BGP picks optimal ISP paths; link failures shift traffic automatically, preserving uptime.
OSPF finds the shortest route and instantly reroutes on failure, keeping the network running.
High Availability
(Shadow mode VRRP)
VRRP links a second UniFi Cloud Gateway in Shadow Mode; if the primary goes offline, the Shadow instantly takes over, ensuring seamless uptime. Additionally enterprise-class gateways use dual hot-swap power supplies for enchanced reliability.
Multi-WAN +
SLAs
UniFi Gateways leverage multiple internet links per site for highly-resilient service. Choose between Failover Only mode using a backup internet link or Distributed mode, splitting traffic across links by customizable weighting.
ISP Health
Monitoring
The UniFi Site Manager dashboard at unifi.ui.com features ISP health metrics for quick, insightful monitoring across sites. Receive real-time email and app alerts for site ISP health, with push notifications settings customizable for each site.
Traffic Flow
Engine
Monitor, visualize, and audit traffic with detailed flow data and advanced logs. UniFi’s real-time records reveal app usage, IP conversations, and protocol behavior. Built-in logs give event-level activity details - ideal for troubleshooting, compliance, and proactive management.
Content
Filtering
Block traffic to over 100 categories of malicious, explicit, or unwanted content from your UniFi Network. With just a few clicks, stop traffic to sites linked to spyware, adult content, social media, and more. Content filtering helps you protect users and track attempts to access restricted sites.
Monitoring & PSA
Integrations (Alarm
Manager)
Automate and scale network monitoring with alarm manager. Set custom thresholds for key metrics like dropped packets, client disconnections and more. Configure automated responses such as push notifications to specific users, create helpdesk tickets via webhook, or send email alerts.
Guest Isolation
Starts Here
Guest WiFi runs on an isolated network - separate from your POS, cameras, and staff - meeting PCI DSS segmentation requirements, on by default.
Frequently Asked Questions
UniFi Assistant
Have a question? Ask UniFi Assistant Anything...
Are there any fees or licenses needed to use a Cloud Gateway?
Absolutely not. We disagree with the current IT industry trend of charging steep licensing fees for cloud platforms or recurring device licenses to unlock or use advanced security features.
As such, UniFi Cloud Gateways offer the best of both worlds:
A completely free remote management experience via our UniFi Portal at
unifi.ui.com
.
The performance and security benefit of an on-premises solution.
The ability to interconnect multiple locations with
Site Magic
, our license-free and scalable SD-WAN.
Intelligent email and mobile push notifications for important system activity.
Out-of-the box support for advanced security features without needing a license.
What kind of Security and Routing features does the Cloud Gateway provide?
Cloud Gateways offer a comprehensive set of advanced security and routing features that are available out-of-the box without the need of a license.
These features include:
License-Free SD-WAN
Signature-Based IPS/IDS Threat Detection
Application-Aware Firewall Rules
Policy-Based WAN and VPN Routing
Redundant WAN with Failover and Load Balancing
WireGuard and OpenVPN Server
OpenVPN and IPsec Site-to-Site VPN
Content, Country, Domain, and Ad Filtering
And much more. See
Tech Specs
for a complete list of features.
What is UniFi OS and which Applications run natively on a Cloud Gateway?
UniFi OS is a powerful and intuitive operating system enabling an end-to-end management experience for all of your IT needs.
The benefits include:
Management from anywhere in the world using our UniFi Site Manager at
unifi.ui.com
, or our UniFi Mobile Apps (
iOS
/
Android
).
Secure multi-factor authentication, including our seamless single-touch solution with UI Verify (
iOS
/
Android
).
Automatic Cloud Backups and Recovery for UniFi OS and UniFi Applications.
The UniFi Applications that can run on a Cloud Gateway are:
UniFi Network:
The core of the network which manages UniFi gateways, switches and access points providing real-time traffic insights and a visual topology.
UniFi Protect:
A constantly evolving, AI-driven camera platform with smart-detection capabilities that records high-quality video to local storage.
UniFi Talk:
A new approach to business telephony offering a plug-and-play phone system and VoIP subscription with powerful call routing and admin features.
UniFi Access:
A state-of-the-art access control system with modern touch-screen access readers, live video, and mobile app credential support.
UniFi Connect:
A modern Enterprise of Things platform that consists of digital signage, lighting, and electric vehicle charging stations.
Note:
UniFi Express
only runs UniFi Network.
How can I migrate my current UniFi deployment to a Cloud Gateway?
Migrating from a different model Cloud Gateway or UniFi Console is straightforward. Simply restore the
System Config Backup
on the new Cloud Gateway.
When migrating from a UniFi Network Server on a Windows/macOS/Linux machine, the UniFi
Network Application Backup
can also be restored on the new Cloud Gateway.
How do I enable Power Redundancy on a Cloud Gateway?
UniFi SmartPower
technology provides resiliency to the Dream Machine Pro Max, Pro, and Special Edition through advanced power redundancy and distribution units. The UniFi Power Backup continuously monitors the Cloud Gateway through the dedicated SmartPower port and provides automatic failover without any interruption in case the primary internal power supply fails.
Visit help.ui.com
